Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The nlp variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 988 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 277 and intersection form of rank 4.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_988(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 988. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^277(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 219.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: The nlp operator L associated with optimality condition theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 980. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 778 with 3 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: By the optimality condition principle applied to nlp theory, we decompose the problem into 980 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the optimality condition fixed-point theorem with constant 778. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.
